# The regions, one by one

*Part of [the electronic diaries of Russia’s regions](../diaries.md).* Every federal subject,
in the order the survey used — republics, krais, oblasts, the federal cities, the autonomous oblast and okrugs — with every platform its schools use or used, the
hosts, the date of the change where there was one, the sentence that says so and the page it
was read on. «Checked» at the end of each block says how far the answer was verified: by an independent
pass told to refute the first, by the deep check the mandatory regions had, and — where it
says so — by the second round that went after the completeness critique's list.
Nothing here was read from a live diary: the evidence is regional portals, ministries, school
sites and news, as of September 2026.

This page is also the input of the region catalog the server and the app read
([how it is built](../diaries.md#the-region-catalog)). `server/scripts/region_catalog.py`
parses each `## ` heading, the *Mandatory region.* line and the system bullets — role,
platform link, hosts, confidence — and nothing else: the «Open-source clients», «Notes.» and
«Checked:» bullets and the evidence indented under each system are for people. A change to a
parsed line needs the catalog regenerated, or `server/tests/test_region_catalog.py` fails.

## Республика Адыгея

- **in use now: [Дневник.ру](dnevnik-ru.md)** — Дневник.ру — РГИС «Цифровая образовательная платформа Республики Адыгея» (ООО «Дневник.ру»). Hosts: `dnevnik.ru`, `login.dnevnik.ru`, `schools.dnevnik.ru`. Confidence: high.
  The regional РГИС branding on Dnevnik.ru's own school profile is what makes this a regional contract rather than the generic per-region ESIA router that login.dnevnik.ru shows for every subject. No 2026/27 announcement of a move to ТОР «Моя школа» or МЭШ could be checked: WebSearch was exhausted in this session and the ministry host mon.adygheya.ru does not resolve (ENOTFOUND); www.adygheya.ru answered 503.
  - https://schools.dnevnik.ru/v2/r/adygea/school?school=1000009641313 — «РГИС «Цифровая образовательная платформа Республики Адыгея» (regional branding on the Dnevnik.ru profile of ГБОУ ДО «Республиканская естественно-математическая школа», opened 2026-09-24)»
  - https://sh29-novaya-adygeya-r79.gosweb.gosuslugi.ru/ — «Чтобы пользоваться услугой «Электронный дневник школьника», вы и ваш ребенок должны быть зарегистрированы на сайте gosuslugi.ru» — the page sends users to authorise on dnevnik.ru
  - https://login.dnevnik.ru/login/esia/adygea — «Республика Адыгея» — «Вам доступен вход в Систему только с учетной записью портала госуслуг»
- **Open-source clients** aimed at this region or its platform: https://github.com/kesha1225/DnevnikRuAPI (Python wrapper for api.dnevnik.ru; region-agnostic.); https://github.com/SiroXYZ/DnevnikRuAPI_NEW (Python; for Dnevnik.ru regions with ESIA-only login.); https://github.com/yasg1988/pydnevnikruapi-extended (Extended Dnevnik.ru API v1/v2 wrapper.)
- **Notes.** Single regional platform, Дневник.ру with ESIA-only login, branded РГИС «Цифровая образовательная платформа Республики Адыгея». Open-source clients listed are the first pass's and were not re-opened here.
- **Checked:** confirmed.

## Республика Алтай

- **in use now: [ТОР «Моя школа»](myschool-federal.md)** — Единая цифровая платформа «Моя школа» (ТОР) — приложение «Госуслуги Моя школа», gosuslugi.ru/school (Federal ТОР «Моя школа» (Минпросвещения / Минцифры России); access via Госуслуги and «Сферум»). Hosts: `gosuslugi.ru/school`. Since: 1 September 2026 (all schools). Confidence: high.
  Full ТОР, not a front end. The Горно-Алтайск gymnasium says the school itself «переходит на работу в Типовом решении «Моя школа»», so the teachers' journal moves too. The city education authority says the diary is available only in «Госуслуги Моя школа» from 1 September 2026. The date has passed and nothing found reports a postponement. The only item dated after 1 September is an aggregator headline (gogov.ru, 17.09.2026). Every *.obr04.ru / *.altaiobr04.ru page answered 503, so no regional page written after 1 September was read. No source names the system being replaced; СГО (sgo.altaiobr04.ru) is the only regional diary on record before it. myschool.edu.ru stays out of hosts (it is the federal portal, fgis_myschool).
  - https://1line.info/news/ak/social/obrazovanie/s-1-sentyabrya-2026-vse-shkoly-respubliki-altay-pereydut-na-moyu-shkolu.html — «13.08.2026: «С 1 сентября 2026 все школы Республики Алтай перейдут на «Мою школу»»; «Разные сервисы и приложения уйдут в прошлое» — access through the app «Госуслуги Моя школа», «Сферум» and gosuslugi.ru/school»
  - https://www.gorno-altaisk.info/news/194497 — «13.08.2026: «Единую цифровую платформу «Моя школа» внедрят в Республике Алтай»; «новая система заменит разные приложения»; access through «портал Госуслуги и коммуникационную среду «Сферум»»»
  - http://muko.edusite.ru/ — «19.08.2026, МУ «Управление образования администрации МО города Горно-Алтайска»: «С 1 сентября 2026 г. электронный дневник будет доступен только в приложении «Госуслуги Моя школа», которое объединит ключевые образовательные сервисы в едином интерфейсе.»»
- **previous: [«Сетевой город»](netschool.md)** — АИС «Сетевой Город. Образование» Республики Алтай (СГО 04) (ИРТех). Hosts: `sgo.altaiobr04.ru`, `poo.altaiobr04.ru`. Since: until 31 August 2026. Confidence: medium.
  sgo.altaiobr04.ru, altaiobr04.ru and the /authorize/login page all answered 503 to WebFetch from here, so no page of the region's own was read. The 1 September 2026 move to ТОР retired СГО as the families' diary: the city authority says the diary is «только в приложении «Госуслуги Моя школа»». No source names СГО explicitly as the system replaced. Whether the server stays readable as an archive is unknown.
  - https://raw.githubusercontent.com/v2fly/domain-list-community/master/data/category-education-ru — «# "Networked city" — an russian automated information system that integrates educational institutions of all types into a single information network» followed by «altaiobr04.ru» (file last changed 30.07.2026, commit «Add a few russian domains (#3506)»)
  - https://raw.githubusercontent.com/daniilok21/Telegram-bot-with-NetSchool-API/main/netschool_cap/netschool_cap/regions.py — «"Республика Алтай": "https://sgo.altaiobr04.ru" (repository last commit 19.06.2026)»
  - https://foedu.ru/respublika-altay.html — «В Республике Алтай присутствуют следующие системы образования АИС Сетевой город» — sgo.altaiobr04.ru, poo.altaiobr04.ru (undated aggregator)
- **previous: [БАРС](bars.md)** — «Барс.Web-Образование» — электронный дневник Республики Алтай (БАРС Груп). Hosts: `sosh.mon-ra.ru`, `spo.mon-ra.ru`. Since: until about 2022. Confidence: medium.
  sosh.mon-ra.ru and mon-ra.ru answered 503. When the region moved from БАРС to Сетевой город was not established. The only evidence is that БАРС was the diary in 2022 and is gone from BARS's directory by 2026.
  - https://raw.githubusercontent.com/mironovmeow/MeowSchool/master/README.MD — «В данный момент поддерживается только sosh.mon-ra.ru (электронный дневник в Республике Алтай, проект Барс.Web-Образование).» (repository last commit 14.04.2022)
  - https://aggregator.edu.bars.group/my_diary — «BARS's own diary directory, opened 2026-09-24, lists 11 regional servers (Владимирская, Вологодская, Кабардино-Балкарская, Магаданская, Мурманская, НАО, Карелия, Хакасия, Ростовская, Удмуртия and a general portal) and no longer lists Республика Алтай or sosh.mon-ra.ru»
- **Open-source clients** aimed at this region or its platform: https://github.com/daniilok21/Telegram-bot-with-NetSchool-API (regions.py maps «Республика Алтай» to https://sgo.altaiobr04.ru (read from raw.githubusercontent.com).); https://github.com/mironovmeow/MeowSchool (VK bot for the legacy БАРС diary sosh.mon-ra.ru (2022, Python).); https://github.com/nm17/netschoolapi (Generic async Python NetSchool client (archived); not Altai-specific.)
- **Notes.** Three generations: БАРС sosh.mon-ra.ru (to about 2022), then Сетевой город sgo.altaiobr04.ru (to 31.08.2026), then ТОР «Моя школа» for all schools from 1 September 2026, covering both the teachers' journal and the families' diary («Госуслуги Моя школа» / Сферум / gosuslugi.ru/school).
- **Corrected during verification.** ТОР row moved from migrating_to to primary (since 1 September 2026, all schools), on two dated sources. The Горно-Алтайск education authority, 19.08.2026 (muko.edusite.ru): «С 1 сентября 2026 г. электронный дневник будет доступен только в приложении «Госуслуги Моя школа»». Гимназия №3 Горно-Алтайска, 12.08.2026 (mougimnazia3.edusite.ru): «переходит на работу в Типовом решении «Моя школа»», so the journal moves, not only the app. The СГО row stays legacy, now dated «until 31 August 2026». БАРС legacy row unchanged. No regional page from after 1 September could be read (503 on every obr04 host); a gogov.ru headline of 17.09.2026 is the only later item.
- **Checked:** corrected in the second round.

## Республика Башкортостан

*Mandatory region.*

- **in use now: [«Моя школа» (МЭШ)](mesh-myschool.md)** — МЭШ / РГИС «Моя школа» (Республика Башкортостан) (ДИТ Москвы (regional МЭШ deployment); mobile app «Моя школа. Дневник» (srgazeta called it «Дневник МЭШ»)). Hosts: `dnevnik.edurb.ru`. Since: 2026/27 (most schools from 1 September 2026, the rest by the end of 2026). Confidence: high.
  WebFetch of https://dnevnik.edurb.ru/ on 2026-09-24 got an empty body (a JS single-page shell, so the host answers), where an earlier pass got 503. The host resolves to 212.45.30.1–3, the addresses of the other 2026 МЭШ nodes (dnevnik.tvobr.ru, dnevnik.edu35.ru, edu.mso51.ru, karelia.minedu.ru). The srgazeta article links the web version as school.mos.ru, which contradicts the official help table (dnevnik.edurb.ru); the table is taken as authoritative. The family app in use from September is «Моя школа. Дневник» (ufa1.ru, 08.09.2026), the DIT regional app, where srgazeta had written «Дневник МЭШ». It is assumed to speak the МЭШ family API (/api/family/web/v1, /api/family/mobile/v1, /api/ej/*) like the other regional instances; only one open-source client actually calls this host. The plan, repeated by the head of the republic on 24.08.2026, is most schools from 1 September and the rest by the end of 2026. The Moscow release of 05.05.2026 had promised all 1,609 schools from the first quarter; the republic's own statements (May–August 2026) put the rest at the end of 2026. No source after 1 September says how many schools have actually switched.
  - https://myschool.mos.ru/help/instructions/mesh-id/authorization-esia/authorization-diaryreg/ — «Regional table: «Республика Башкортостан \| https://dnevnik.edurb.ru»; for parents and pupils: «Нажмите на кнопку «Войти через Госуслуги»»»
  - https://www.mos.ru/city/projects/mesh/ — «С первой четверти 2026/2027 учебного года к «МЭШ» присоединяются еще пять новых субъектов России. Среди них – Республики Башкортостан и Карелия, Вологодская, Мурманская и Тверская области.»
  - https://srgazeta.ru/articles/obrazovanie/2026-08-24/s-1-sentyabrya-shkoly-bashkirii-prisoedinyatsya-k-moskovskoy-elektronnoy-shkole-4790758 — «24.08.2026: «С 1 сентября 2026 года свыше 2 000 школ Башкортостана подключатся к цифровой экосистеме»; «большая часть школ Башкортостана перейдёт на МЭШ с 1 сентября, остальные — до конца 2026 года»; «мобильное приложение «Дневник МЭШ»»; «Для авторизации требуется подтверждённая учётная запись на Госуслугах.»»
- **alongside: [ELSCHOOL](elschool.md)** — АИС «Образование» Республики Башкортостан / «Электронная школа ELSCHOOL» (Башкирский регистр социальных карт (БРСК); JWT audience it.brsc.ru). Hosts: `elschool.ru`, `api.elschool.ru`, `file.elschool.ru`, `edu.brsc.ru`. Since: primary until 2025/26; from 1 September 2026 kept only by the schools not yet moved to МЭШ, which are due to move by the end of 2026. Confidence: medium.
  Now named as the system МЭШ replaces: pupils' data were carried from Elschool (АИС «Образование») into МЭШ in summer 2026 (ufa1.ru, 08.07.2026). Role is secondary rather than legacy because the move is phased. By the plan repeated on 24.08.2026, the schools that did not switch on 1 September stay on Elschool until they do, by the end of 2026. The Moscow release of 05.05.2026 had instead promised МЭШ in «все 1609 школ республики» from the first quarter, so how long the overlap lasts rests on the republic's later statements. Which schools those are, how many, and when elschool.ru stops serving schools or turns into an archive: no source read says, before or after 1 September. elschool.ru still resolves (185.235.72.106, 185.235.72.125) but answered 503 to WebFetch on 2026-09-24, as in every earlier pass. Wayback was offline for part of this round. edu.brsc.ru is an older alias that 301-redirects to elschool.ru (Wayback, June 2023). Sign-in: login + password issued by the school (POST /Logon/Index → JWToken cookie, optional GoogleAuthCode TOTP field) or «Госуслуги» (ЕСИА client BRSC-GOSUSLUGI_ID-GATEWAY via esia.brsc.ru). The api.elschool.ru JSON API is known only from the 2022 aioelschools package.
  - https://kaltschool1.ru/%D1%8D%D0%BB%D0%B5%D0%BA%D1%82%D1%80%D0%BE%D0%BD%D0%BD%D1%8B%D0%B9-%D0%B6%D1%83%D1%80%D0%BD%D0%B0%D0%BB-2/ — «С помощью электронного дневника БРСК в системе АИС «Образование» можно смотреть оценки, расписание и домашнее задание.»; «Электронная школа ELSCHOOL» — https://elschool.ru/, https://edu.brsc.ru/Logon/Index
  - https://github.com/Ramin2009Mc/Elschool-Parser — «Scraper with commits up to 29 November 2025 still logging in at elschool.ru/Logon/Index and reading /users/diaries — the platform was live in 2025/26»
  - https://ufa1.ru/text/education/2026/07/08/76522494/ — «08.07.2026: teachers report that «персональные данные обучающихся передаются из системы Elschool (АИС „Образование". — прим. ред.) в систему МЭШ»; administrators trained «с 20 июля по 7 августа», teachers from 10 August»
- **Open-source clients** aimed at this region or its platform: https://github.com/SolomonNumb1/fgis-dnevnik-desktop (Electron/React/TypeScript desktop client reverse-engineered from the official app ru.mes.dnevnik.fgis (last commit 13 September 2026). Region table: «Республика Башкортостан» dnevnik.edurb.ru (the DEFAULT region of the client and of its ЕСИА login window), «Республика Дагестан» myschool.05edu.ru, «Республика Татарстан» ms-edu.tatar.ru; calls https://<region>/api/family/web/v1/{profile,schedule,subject_marks,homeworks,attendance,visits,food/meals}, /api/ej/rating/v1/rank/*, /api/materials/v1/{uuid} with Bearer token + cookie aupd_token and x-mes-subsystem: familyweb; token captured from the ЕСИА redirect in an embedded window. Many other rows of its 83-region table (myschool.<region> hosts) look guessed.); https://github.com/Ilya-Repin/elschool-api (C++ (userver) service parsing grades from elschool.ru; last commit March 2024.); https://github.com/Ilya-Repin/Elschooler (Go rewrite of elschool-api with results table parsing; last commit May 2025.); https://github.com/Ramin2009Mc/Elschool-Parser (Python parser: decodes JWToken claims for ids, grades and diary; last commit November 2025.); https://github.com/Armagagedone/Elschool-Parser-Library (Python library: personal data, diary week pages, class-year switch, logout; last commit September 2024.); https://github.com/Kiera38/elschool_bot (Python Telegram bot for elschool grades; last commit August 2024.); https://github.com/Kiera38/elschooltgbot (Earlier Python Telegram bot by the same author (April 2023); tests carry a sample JWToken.); https://github.com/code-1080/elschool_parsing_bot (Python aiohttp bot: login, JWT decode, diary and grades; last commit May 2025.)
- **Notes.** Sign-in (2026/27, МЭШ): Госуслуги/ЕСИА only, with a verified account — «Войти через Госуслуги» on dnevnik.edurb.ru; no school-issued login documented. An API client must obtain the МЭШ bearer token from the ЕСИА redirect in a browser/WebView (as fgis-dnevnik-desktop does). Legacy elschool: school-issued login/password or Госуслуги. Mobile app: «Моя школа. Дневник», running from the start of 2026/27 per ufa1.ru, 08.09.2026 (the DIT МЭШ app with a region picker, ru.mes.dnevnik.fgis; srgazeta had called it «Дневник МЭШ»); no official API documentation for either platform. Searches with nothing new: GitHub repositories «edurb», «bashkortostan school», topic «elschool» (unused), «elschool api» (one hit); Sourcegraph for dnevnik.edurb.ru, edurb.ru, elschool.ru (no client code). WebSearch budget was exhausted in both attempts. This round searched Google News RSS, decoded from Bash, and Brave when it answered; there was nothing on elschool after 1 September.
- **Corrected during verification.** The elschool row changed from legacy to secondary, as the critic asked. The rollout is phased: «большинство школ ... с 1 сентября, остальные — до конца этого года» (head of the republic, okt-neft.ru, 24.08.2026). ufa1.ru (08.07.2026) names Elschool (АИС «Образование») as the system whose data move into МЭШ. Its since now reads «primary until 2025/26; for schools not yet moved until the end of 2026», with three new evidence items. The mesh row gained three items: the 24.08.2026 quote; ufa1.ru (08.09.2026), which confirms that the app «Моя школа. Дневник» runs from the start of the year; and the Moscow release of 05.05.2026, whose «все 1609 школ республики» from the first quarter conflicts with the republic's phased plan. The app name is corrected to «Моя школа. Дневник» in the mesh row's vendor and notes and in the region notes. Still unresolved: which schools remain on elschool.ru and when it closes. No source before or after 1 September names them, and elschool.ru answers 503 from here.
- **Checked:** deep check, second round.

## Республика Бурятия

- **in use now: [«Сетевой город»](netschool.md)** — ГИС «Электронное образование Республики Бурятия» — подсистема «Сетевой город. Образование» (ИС «Контингент») (ИРТех). Hosts: `deti.obr03.ru`. Since: 2016/17 (implementation from September 2016, pupils from January 2017; regulation approved by Government decree of 01.12.2016 № 549). Confidence: high.
  deti.obr03.ru answered 403 to WebFetch. The republic's portal search (egov-buryatia.ru) for «Госуслуги Моя школа», «Моя школа», «электронный журнал 2026» found no announcement of a move to ТОР or МЭШ; its only 2025–2026 hit is a 24.01.2025 note that regional data showcases were built «в целях отображения указанных сведений на Госуслугах и их предоставления в федеральную систему «Моя школа»», which is the ФГИС data feed, not a new diary.
  - https://egov-buryatia.ru/obr/index.php — «Отчет по достижению указанного Показателя формируется в подсистеме «Сетевой город. Образования»» — the system is at deti.obr03.ru; regulation of the «Электронное образование Республики Бурятия» system approved by decree of 01.12.2016 № 549 (Government of Buryatia portal)
  - https://egov-buryatia.ru/minobr/projects/about_the_projects/kontingent/index.php — «Система «Контингент» позволяет ученикам и их родителям через Интернет просмотреть отметки по предметам, домашние задания, сообщения от учителей, расписание занятий»; «создание и внедрение системы началось в сентябре 2016 года»
  - https://maou-4m.buryatschool.ru/?section_id=4 — «С 2017-2018 уч.года в нашей школе осуществлен переход на электронный журнал в ИС "Контингент".»
- **alongside: [ЭлЖур](eljur.md)** — ЭлЖур (individual schools in Улан-Удэ) (ЭлЖур). Hosts: `maou-37.eljur.ru`. Since: 2020. Confidence: low.
  A school-level instance beside the regional СГО, not a region-wide platform. uu20.eljur.ru from the first pass was not re-opened and is left out.
  - https://maou-37.buryatschool.ru/?section_id=5 — «В сентябре 2020 года в школе внедряется система электронного журнала "ЭЛЖУР" (ЭЖ).» — «Ссылка на электронный журнал» https://maou-37.eljur.ru/
- **Open-source clients** aimed at this region or its platform: https://github.com/mezhendosina/che-zadali-app (Android NetSchool client; its netschoolEsia Regions.kt lists «Республика Бурятия» → https://deti.obr03.ru/ (checked in a local clone).); https://github.com/daniilok21/Telegram-bot-with-NetSchool-API (regions.py maps «Республика Бурятия» to https://deti.obr03.ru.); https://github.com/kamekuro/nsbot-v2 (NetSchool bot said by the first pass to map RESP_BUR to deti.obr03.ru; not re-opened here.)
- **Notes.** Regional СГО at deti.obr03.ru, run as a subsystem of the republic's «Электронное образование»; one Ulan-Ude school on ЭлЖур; no sign of a 2026 move.
- **Checked:** confirmed.

## Республика Дагестан

*Mandatory region.*

- **in use now: [«Моя школа» (МЭШ)](mesh-myschool.md)** — «Электронное образование Дагестана (МЭШ)» / РГИС «Моя школа» (ДИТ Москвы (regional МЭШ deployment)). Hosts: `myschool.05edu.ru`, `education.05edu.ru`. Since: 2024/25 (pilot from October 2023; all schools from 1 September 2024). Confidence: high.
  WebFetch of https://myschool.05edu.ru/ on 2026-09-24 returned an empty body (JS shell; host answers). education.05edu.ru is from the app string table only; role unverified. ## Донецкая Народная Республика

- **in use now: [ТОР «Моя школа»](myschool-federal.md)** — ТОР «Моя школа» / «Госуслуги. Моя школа» (regional rollout «Цифровое образование ДНР») (Federal ТОР «Моя школа» (типовое облачное решение); login through Госуслуги (ЕСИА) and the messenger MAX). Hosts: `gosuslugi.ru/school`. Since: 1 September 2026 (опытная/тестовая эксплуатация; paper journals kept alongside). Confidence: medium.
  The teachers' journal and the families' diary are both ТОР: the full typical cloud solution, not a front end. The DNR is one of the 20 first-wave ТОР regions (densus headline, 31.08.2026). ТОР is the republic's only regional electronic diary and has been live since 1 September 2026, but officially in test operation: on 03.09.2026 paper journals and diaries were kept, and the electronic service was called «дополнительным инструментом». Confidence is medium because what ran in September is described only by news items, one of them a headline read from a feed, and not by the ministry. What ТОР replaces is paper, not a regional electronic system. myschool.edu.ru stays out of hosts.

  - https://sch2aseg.gosuslugi.ru/pedagogam-i-sotrudnikam/ — «С июля 2026 года школы переходят на новый цифровой образовательный сервис ТОР «Моя школа». … Что нужно сделать родителям до 1 сентября 2026 года? (Алчевская социально-экономическая гимназия, ЛНР)»
  - https://t.me/minobrlnr/20396 — «С 1 сентября 2026 года электронный дневник будет доступен только в мобильном приложении «Госуслуги Моя школа». (Минобрнауки ЛНР, 11.08.2026; repeated 19.08.2026 in post 20441)»
- **previous: [ЭлЖур](eljur.md)** — РГИС «Террикон» Луганской Народной Республики на платформе ЭлЖур («Электронный журнал ЭлЖур»), per-school instances lnrNNNN.eljur.ru (ООО «Веб-Мост» (ЭлЖур)). Hosts: `lnr0487.eljur.ru`, `lnr0523.eljur.ru`, `lnr0488.eljur.ru`, `*.eljur.ru`. Since: 2024/25 pilots; all schools 1 September 2025; replaced by ТОР from July 2026. Confidence: high.
  The vendor names the regional ГИС «Террикон» as its own system (ЭлЖур news, October 2025); school sites give per-school lnrNNNN.eljur.ru hosts. This is what the ТОР notices call «старое региональное приложение». Fate: unknown — the ministry says only that the diary is available solely in the federal app from 1 September 2026; whether the lnrNNNN.eljur.ru instances were switched off or kept is not stated.
  - https://sh28-lugansk-r181.gosweb.gosuslugi.ru/ — «Для работы ГБОУ ЛНР "ЛСШ №28" используется информационная система «Электронный журнал ЭлЖур», соответствующая требованиям Министерства образования и науки. (address lnr0487.eljur.ru)»
  - https://lug-info.ru/news/nabor-dopolnitelnyh-programm-vojdet-v-elektronnyj-zhurnal--minobrnauki-lnr/ — «24.01.2025: «с 1 сентября 2025 года новая электронная система начнет функционировать во всех учебных учреждениях Республики.» (the ministry does not name the vendor)»
  - https://eljur.ru/news/2025_lnr — «15.10.2025, ООО «Веб-Мост»: «Модернизация региональной государственной информационной системы «Террикон» Луганской Народной Республики» — «Внедрение модуля для работы дошкольных образовательных организаций»»
- **Open-source clients** aimed at this region or its platform: https://github.com/Statuxia-API-Eljur-ru/API-Eljur-ru (Platform-generic ЭлЖур Python client; relevant to the 2025/26 lnrNNNN.eljur.ru instances only.); https://github.com/BetterJournal/EljurAPI (Unofficial ЭлЖур API documentation/wrapper; platform-generic.)
- **Notes.** РГИС «Террикон» on ЭлЖур (lnrNNNN.eljur.ru) in 2025/26 → ТОР «Моя школа» / «Госуслуги Моя школа» from 1 September 2026, a pilot region on the official list. No LNR-specific client exists for either platform, and none was found for «Госуслуги Моя школа» (its web entry is a JS shell behind ЕСИА).
- **Corrected during verification.** ТОР confidence raised from medium to high: the republic's ministry itself announced it (t.me/minobrlnr/20396, 11.08.2026; 20623, 31.08.2026), the official Госуслуги list names the ЛНР, and ЛИЦ (08.09.2026, citing Минцифры ЛНР) describes «Моя школа» as the system children must have an account for. Roles, kinds and hosts unchanged; the ministry channel carries no further September post on the diary.
- **Checked:** corrected in the second round.

In use in September 2026: eleven Владикавказ and Беслан school profiles read on 24.09.2026 carry September 2026 activity ratings of 2,463–30,590, and new schools kept joining (МБОУ СОШ №9 г.Беслана in September 2024, ОЦ «Новый город» in September 2025, both in the platform's new-schools ranking). ## Республика Тыва

- **in use now: [Дневник.ру](dnevnik-ru.md)** — Цифровая образовательная платформа «Дневник.ру» (электронный журнал и дневник Республики Тыва) (ООО «Дневник.ру»). Hosts: `dnevnik.ru`, `17.dnevnik.ru`, `login.dnevnik.ru/login/esia/tuva`, `schools.dnevnik.ru`. Since: by June 2023 (after БАРС); Дневник.ру was also used in 2011–2012. Confidence: high.
  In use in 2025/26 and September 2026: six Кызыл and Каа-Хем school profiles read on 24.09.2026 carry September 2026 activity ratings of 3,198–12,575, and Tyvan schools were still being connected in September 2024 and September–October 2025 (new-schools ranking). Schools in regions that left Дневник.ру for ТОР «Моя школа» on 1 September 2026 read 17–157 on the same day (Воронеж: Лицей №1 45, СОШ №37 17, лицей № 4 157; Астрахань СОШ № 28 86 with 2233 pupils; Биробиджан Гимназия №1 57 with 1519 pupils), so a rating in the thousands means the journal is being kept there this September. The profiles carry no regional РГИС branding. minobr.rtyva.ru (403) and ioko.rtyva.ru (503) could not be read.

## Запорожская область

- **in use now: [«one.» platform](one-x1.md)** — РИС «Цифровое образование Запорожской области», подсистема «Электронные журналы и дневники» (ООО «Интегрикс» «X1» platform (the one.<domain> + passport.<domain> code base of Псковская/Кировская/Чукотка), apparently supplied through the Pskov РЦИТ (Запорожская experts at its April 2025 forum on system integration); operator Министерство образования и науки Запорожской области. No vendor statement names Интегрикс for this instance). Hosts: `one.umnik-zo.ru`, `passport.umnik-zo.ru`. Since: by 2025 (user manuals dated 2025). Confidence: high.
  Primary in 2025/26 and, as far as found, 2026/27; the gosweb hosts ending in -r23 are Запорожская область schools. one.umnik-zo.ru and passport.umnik-zo.ru answered 403 and umnik-zo.ru 503 to WebFetch. Kind one_x1: the official parent manual shows the X1 sign-in «Войти через passport.umnik-zo.ru» and the X1 parent functions «Дневник учащегося», «Выписка оценок», «Дневник в xls»; the one_x1 route file lists one.umnik-zo.ru as CONFIRMED. DNS on 2026-09-24 (no wildcard: a random name under umnik-zo.ru does not resolve): one.umnik-zo.ru and passport.umnik-zo.ru → 213.145.5.56, wp.umnik-zo.ru → 213.145.5.58 — the MegaFon /24 that also holds Pskov's one.pskovedu.ru (.41) and Kherson's one.edu.khogov.ru / passport.edu.khogov.ru (.57) and wp.edu.khogov.ru (.59), which points to the three instances being hosted together, by the Pskov РЦИТ. 2026/27: the federal statement excludes Запорожская область from using «Госуслуги Моя школа» alongside the regional system, so the regional system remains the only electronic diary; no source found that it was stopped.
  - https://sh3-velikaya-znamenka-r23.gosweb.gosuslugi.ru/ — «Для регистрации в электронном дневнике авторизуйтесь на сайте https://one.umnik-zo.ru и заполните онлайн-форму» (ГБУ ОО ЗО «Великознаменская СОШ № 3» Каменско-Днепровского района; re-opened 2026-09-24)
  - https://sh2-konstantinov-r23.gosweb.gosuslugi.ru/netcat_files/38/128/Rukovodstvo_pol_zovatelya_Roditel_.pdf — «Министерство образования и науки Запорожской области … Региональная информационная система «Цифровое образование Запорожской области» Подсистема «Электронные журналы и дневники» … 2025» … «На странице авторизации нажмите «Войти через passport.umnik-zo.ru».» … «В файл xls можно также скачать и сам дневник, для этого нажмите «Дневник в xls»» (text of the parent manual as saved earlier in this research; read in full here)
  - https://github.com/zlexdev/pskovedu-sdk — «`pskovedu` is a fully typed, async-first Python client for the **one.pskovedu.ru** electronic journal» … «# Marks report (выписка оценок)» … Client.from_cookie(x1_sso="...") — the same one./passport. platform and «выписка оценок» function (last commit 2026-06-20)
- **Open-source clients** aimed at this region or its platform: https://github.com/zlexdev/pskovedu-sdk (Python client for the same platform family on one.pskovedu.ru (X1_SSO cookie, /edv diary, marks report); not tested against one.umnik-zo.ru); https://github.com/Mihail-Galkin/Two.Diary (client for the Kirov instance (one.43edu.ru / passport.43edu.ru) of the same platform family; not Zaporozhye-specific)
- **Notes.** 2025/26 and 2026/27: РИС «Цифровое образование Запорожской области» at one.umnik-zo.ru (sign-in via passport.umnik-zo.ru with СНИЛС + password, or ЕСИА), on the Интегрикс X1 platform. The platform before 2025 is unresolved. Not in ТОР: the official «Госуслуги для родителей» statement (31.08.2026) and densus.ru (10.09.2026) name Запорожская and Херсонская as the two regions where «Госуслуги Моя школа» is not even an option. The ministry's own site and channel were not found (zo.gov.ru has no link; ten guessed Telegram names empty); Google News RSS («umnik-zo», «Запорожская область электронный дневник 2026», «Минобрнауки Запорожской области электронный журнал») returned nothing on the diary. RuStore search (backapi.rustore.ru, «Цифровое образование Запорожской области») shows no regional app.
- **Corrected during verification.** Primary row: kind other → one_x1 and confidence medium → high. The platform rests on the official parent manual (passport.umnik-zo.ru sign-in, «Выписка оценок», «Дневник в xls») that the one_x1 route file counts as CONFIRMED, and now also on DNS: one./passport./wp.umnik-zo.ru sit at 213.145.5.56/.58, next to Kherson's X1 hosts (.57/.59) that came from the Pskov РЦИТ. Vendor now names the Интегрикс platform, with the vendor attribution still inferred. Added densus.ru of 10.09.2026: «В остальных регионах, кроме Запорожской и Херсонской областей, приложением можно пользоваться наравне с другими решениями» — so no tor_myschool row is added.

- **in use now: [Дневник.ру](dnevnik-ru.md)** — Дневник.ру (ООО «Дневник.ру»). Hosts: `dnevnik.ru`, `37.dnevnik.ru`, `login.dnevnik.ru/login/esia/ivanovskaya`, `schools.dnevnik.ru`. Since: December 2020. Confidence: high.
  Primary since December 2020; the regional entry host still routes to an ESIA-only Ivanovo sign-in on 24 September 2026, and five Иваново school profiles read that day carry September 2026 activity ratings of 9,990–18,702. Schools in regions that left Дневник.ру for ТОР «Моя школа» on 1 September 2026 read 17–157 on the same day (Воронеж: Лицей №1 45, СОШ №37 17, лицей № 4 157; Астрахань СОШ № 28 86 with 2233 pupils; Биробиджан Гимназия №1 57 with 1519 pupils), so a rating in the thousands means the journal is being kept there this September. www.dnevnik37.ru is an information page, not an official host. Ивановская is not among the МЭШ regions (ng.ru, 07.05.2026).

*Mandatory region.*

- **in use now: [«Моя школа» (МЭШ)](mesh-myschool.md)** — РГИС «Моя школа» Калужской области (МЭШ-based electronic journal and diary) (ДИТ Москвы / «Центр Цифровизации Образования» (МЭШ platform); operator Министерство образования и науки Калужской области). Hosts: `education.admoblkaluga.ru`, `dnevnik.admoblkaluga.ru`. Since: 2023/24 (from the second quarter, i.e. from about 1 November 2023; ФГИС «Моя школа» pilot region with МЭШ services from 2022). Confidence: high.
  Stays on the МЭШ-based platform in 2026/27 and adds colleges from 1 September 2026. The school launch is now dated: families were moved from the old diary in the second quarter of 2023/24, around 1 November 2023, with entry at education.admoblkaluga.ru and sign-in through Госуслуги (Знамя, 07.11.2023; Калужский перекрёсток, 11.11.2023). Before that, from 2022, Kaluga was a pilot region of ФГИС «Моя школа» built on МЭШ services (Вести образования, 07.09.2023). Host roles per OctoDiary-kt's interface table: education.admoblkaluga.ru = regional auth + secondary API, dnevnik.admoblkaluga.ru = diary/session, api(.admoblkaluga.ru) = main school API; dnevnik.* now only redirects.

Образование», /webapi/logindata reports version 5.56.4 with school login, e-mail login and ЕСИА sign-in all enabled, and /webapi/prepareloginform still lists the whole region, including general-education schools. So the old diary has not been switched off, and families can presumably still open 2025/26 and earlier marks there. Образование","version":"5.56.4","schoolLogin":true,"emLogin":true,"esiaLogin":true,"esiaLoginPage":"/webapi/sso/esia/crosslogin","esiaMainAuth":true,...} — the server is up and still accepts sign-in, ЕСИА included»
- **Open-source clients** aimed at this region or its platform: https://github.com/mezhendosina/che-zadali-app (Kotlin Android client for «Сетевой город. Образование» (NetSchool). Regions.kt maps «Тверская область» → https://sgo.tvobr.ru/. Last commit 2024-05-24. Legacy system.); https://github.com/vkrya/netschoolpy (Python NetSchool library (PyPI netschoolpy 8.0.2). regions.py maps «Тверская область» → https://sgo.tvobr.ru. Legacy system.); https://github.com/daniilok21/Telegram-bot-with-NetSchool-API (Python Telegram bot. netschool_cap/regions.py (copied from netschoolpy) maps «Тверская область» → https://sgo.tvobr.ru. Last commit 2026-06-19. Legacy system.); https://github.com/nm17/netschoolapi (Python async NetSchool /webapi client. Generic: takes any SGO host, so sgo.tvobr.ru works by URL. No Tver-specific code.); https://github.com/OctoDiary/OctoDiary (Kotlin Multiplatform МЭШ-family client. Its RegionCode enum has no Tver entry, and supportedRegionCodes is Moscow, MosReg and Tatarstan. Useful as a reference for the МЭШ API that dnevnik.tvobr.ru serves, not as a working Tver client.)
- **Notes.** 2025/26: «Сетевой Город. Образование» at sgo.tvobr.ru (ИРТех). 2026/27: the МЭШ-based regional platform, entry https://dnevnik.tvobr.ru/esia/. Official МЭШ help lists Тверская область among the 11 regions of «Авторизация для учащихся и родителей в регионах». Sign-in on МЭШ: ЕСИА/Госуслуги only for pupils and parents, which is the /esia/ entry. No regional login or password was found. No source opened says how people signed in to the old sgo.tvobr.ru. che-zadali-app keeps it in its netschoolEsia region list, which suggests ЕСИА sign-in there as well. Mobile app: the DIT «Моя школа Дневник» (RuStore ru.mes.dnevnik.fgis, publisher «Центр Цифровизации Образования», v3.87.8 of 13.09.2026) asks the user to choose a region. The brief states Tver is in that picker, but the store listing names no regions and no Tver host was found in the decompiled 3.79.13 bundle (AmetistYT/mesh_expressive), so this is unconfirmed. Open-source search: GitHub repository search for 'tvobr' found 3 unrelated repositories. Sourcegraph 'tvobr.ru' found only VPN/domain lists (fptn, v2fly domain-list-community). No client references dnevnik.tvobr.ru. There is no official API documentation. A client for the МЭШ API shape (/api/family/web/v1, /api/family/mobile/v1, /v3/auth/esia/login) should work by swapping the host.

The netschool row keeps kind and role (legacy for the school diary, because September press says all schools moved). It gains this evidence, a since that says the server still runs, and notes on what it still carries. No shutdown or archive notice was found, and no source shows a school still keeping its current journal there, so that part of the critic's point stays open. ## Тюменская область

*Mandatory region.*

- **in use now: [«Моя школа» (МЭШ)](mesh-myschool.md)** — «Моя школа» Тюменская область — МЭШ-based regional platform (AIS «Электронная школа Тюменской области») (Департамент информационных технологий города Москвы (МЭШ platform); regional operator Тюменская область (72to.ru)). Hosts: `myschool.72to.ru`, `school.72to.ru`, `education.72to.ru`. Since: 2024/25 (mobile app from 29 August 2024; web diary fully working from 11 September 2024). Confidence: high.
  Confirmed on the official МЭШ help page «Авторизация для учащихся и родителей в регионах» (Тюменская область — https://myschool.72to.ru/). The decompiled official app ru.mes.dnevnik 3.79.13 (AmetistYT/mesh_expressive) sets prod apiBaseUrl https://myschool.72to.ru and the family API https://myschool.72to.ru/api/family/mobile, with education.72to.ru as a further host of unknown role. The web entry used in regional news is https://myschool.72to.ru/72/. The old host school.72to.ru redirects to myschool.72to.ru (Wayback, October 2024). For 2026/27 the platform continues and colleges join in 2027. No move to ТОР «Моя школа» has been announced. myschool.72to.ru is an SPA (WebFetch saw no text). Host roles: myschool.72to.ru is the МЭШ node (DNS 94.79.51.172–174, the addresses shared with school.yanao.ru, myschool.05edu.ru and education.admoblkaluga.ru). school.72to.ru belongs to this row only as a name that redirects here: it answered 301 from 29 August 2024 on (Wayback CDX) and t-l.ru announced the new platform at that address. It still resolves to 109.233.225.75, the regional server that also carries old-school.72to.ru, not to the МЭШ cluster. sd.72to.ru is the regional technical-support portal, not a diary.
  - https://myschool.mos.ru/help/instructions/mesh-id/authorization-esia/authorization-diaryreg/ — «Official МЭШ help, region table «Авторизация для учащихся и родителей в регионах»: «Тюменская область — https://myschool.72to.ru/»»
  - https://www.mos.ru/city/projects/mesh/ — «С 2022 года подсистемы и цифровые сервисы «Московской электронной школы» выступают основой для региональных образовательных платформ. Они уже работают в Калужской, Московской, Тюменской областях, республиках Татарстан и Дагестан, Ямало-Ненецком автономном округе.»
  - https://72.ru/text/education/2024/09/11/74073659/ — «11.09.2024: «С сегодняшнего дня в Тюменской области начало полноценно работать приложение «Моя школа»»; «Электронный дневник и приложение «Образование72» перестали работать с 29 августа.»»
- **previous: [БАРС](bars.md)** — АИС / РЕГИСО «Электронная школа Тюменской области» on БАРС «Электронная школа» (app «Образование 72») (БАРС Груп). Hosts: `old-school.72to.ru`. Since: ≈2013 until 29 August 2024. Confidence: medium.
  The 2023 login page has the БАРС «Электронная школа» layout: /auth/login-page, /static/auth/, and the «БАРС.UP» SSO path /auth/barsup/send-authn-request (the Wayback CDX also has /personal-area/ and /desk). That, with the dnvk.ru listing, supports the БАРС attribution, though no page names the vendor outright. Sign-in was ЕСИА for parents, plus login and password for staff. The legacy app «Образование 72» (Google Play com.education72, App Store id957954921) stopped on 29 August 2024. Hosts: school.72to.ru was this system's address until 29 August 2024 (the dnvk.ru and school-link evidence above refer to that period); since then it only redirects to the МЭШ node, so it is listed under mesh_myschool and not here. old-school.72to.ru is kept as the reported archive host of «Электронная школа Тюменской области»: it resolves to 109.233.225.75, the same regional server as school.72to.ru, but answered 503 to WebFetch and HTTP on 2026-09-24 and has no Wayback capture, so whether it still opens past marks is unverified.

  - https://web.archive.org/web/20230616105940id_/https://school.72to.ru/auth/login-page — «<title>Электронная школа Тюменской области; «подсистеме РЕГИСО «Электронная школа»»; «Вход в Систему «Электронная школа Тюменской области» осуществляется с использованием учетной записи портала Госуслуг»; «Для успешной авторизации в мобильном приложении «Образование 72» необходимо сначала пройти авторизацию по ЕСИА в веб-версии»; SSO link /auth/barsup/send-authn-request; Google Play com.education72»

The best public source of the regional base URLs.); https://github.com/OctoDiary/OctoDiary (Kotlin Multiplatform МЭШ-family client. RegionCode.Tumen("72") is in the region picker, but supportedRegionCodes covers only Moscow, MosReg and Tatarstan, so Tyumen is listed and not usable. Last commit 2026-05-12.); https://github.com/SolomonNumb1/fgis-dnevnik-desktop (TypeScript desktop МЭШ/«Моя школа» client. src/services/api.ts lists { id: '72', name: 'Тюменская область', domain: 'myschool.72to.ru' }, the correct host, though the rest of its 80-region table is unverified.)

Before, until 29.08.2024: БАРС «Электронная школа» at school.72to.ru with the app «Образование 72», which now 301-redirects to myschool.72to.ru. Sign-in: Госуслуги/ЕСИА only (72.ru: «авторизоваться через «Госуслуги»»). The parent's Госуслуги «Семья и дети» must hold the child, with confirmed accounts for both. The mobile app lets the user set a local password after the first ЕСИА login. Mobile app: «Моя школа. Дневник» / «Моя школа» (the DIT МЭШ app, ru.mes.dnevnik.fgis in RuStore) in RuStore, App Store, RuMarket, AppGallery and Google Play, where the user picks Тюменская область. There is no official API documentation. The API is the МЭШ family API at https://myschool.72to.ru/api/family/mobile (and /api/family/web). Open-source search: GitHub repository search for '72to' found only an unrelated archive scraper (and-semakin/archive-image-downloader for tobarhiv.72to.ru). GitHub search for '"Образование 72" OR education72 OR "Электронная школа Тюменской"' found 0. Sourcegraph for '72to.ru' and 'education72' found only domain lists (infoculture/govdomains and others).

The bars row keeps old-school.72to.ru as the reported archive host only. It resolves to the same regional server, 109.233.225.75, but answered 503 and has no Wayback capture, so its archive role is noted as unverified. Added the CDX evidence and a vsluh.ru item naming «Моя школа Дневник» and myschool.72to.ru/72 to the mesh row. The DNS split is recorded in the notes: myschool.72to.ru sits on the МЭШ cluster 94.79.51.172–174, while school.72to.ru and old-school.72to.ru sit on 109.233.225.75. - **in use now: [«one.» platform](one-x1.md)** — РГИС «Цифровое образование Херсонской области» (РГИС «ЦОХО»), «Электронные журналы и дневники» (developed by ГБУ ПО «Региональный центр информационных технологий» (Псковская область) on the ООО «Интегрикс» «X1» platform (the same one.<domain> + passport.<domain> code base as one.pskovedu.ru); operator Министерство образования и науки Херсонской области; regional centre ГКУ Херсонской области «Региональный центр образования» (rco.edu.khogov.ru)). Hosts: `one.edu.khogov.ru`, `passport.edu.khogov.ru`, `wp.edu.khogov.ru`. Since: 2025/26 (system handed over free of charge by the Pskov РЦИТ in April 2025; put into operation by ministry order № 904 of 05.08.2025; deployment first announced in April 2024). Confidence: high.
  Host from the ministry's own page (06.04.2026). passport.edu.khogov.ru and wp.edu.khogov.ru: the first is seen only in DNS, the second is named by the user agreement; both, like one.edu.khogov.ru, are explicit DNS records (a random name under edu.khogov.ru does not resolve, while *.khogov.ru is a wildcard to 185.178.208.132): one./passport.edu.khogov.ru → 213.145.5.57, wp.edu.khogov.ru → 213.145.5.59, next to Zaporozhye's one./passport.umnik-zo.ru (.56) and wp.umnik-zo.ru (.58) and in the same MegaFon /24 as Pskov's one.pskovedu.ru (.41). Kind one_x1 on the official statement that the system was developed and handed over by the Pskov РЦИТ (whose own system is the X1 platform, apps ru.integrics.*), the one./passport./wp. host set shared with the other X1 instances, and the СНИЛС-based registration. one.edu.khogov.ru and passport.edu.khogov.ru answered 503 to WebFetch, so no /edv route was seen. No mobile app found (RuStore search API and App Store search on 2026-09-24).

Not in ТОР: the official federal statement (31.08.2026) and densus.ru (10.09.2026) name Херсонская and Запорожская as the two regions where «Госуслуги Моя школа» is not an option. Found through the WordPress search API of edu.khogov.ru (/wp-json/wp/v2/search); crt.sh answered 502 and Cert Spotter shows only rco.edu.khogov.ru and edu.khogov.ru certificates (the diary hosts carry no publicly logged certificate). WebSearch budget was spent before this pass.
- **Corrected during verification.** Hostname found: the ministry's page «Отчеты» (edu.khogov.ru/deyatelnost/otchety/, 06.04.2026) links «Электронные журналы и дневники https://one.edu.khogov.ru/auth/login»; the user agreement (29.08.2025) names wp.edu.khogov.ru; passport.edu.khogov.ru resolves beside them. Kind other → one_x1 on the ministry's statement of 22.04.2025 (t.me/s/depobrherson/12987) that the РГИС was developed and handed over by the Pskov РЦИТ, and on the one./passport./wp. host set on addresses next to Zaporozhye's X1 instance. since changed from «2024/25» to 2025/26 on order № 904 of 05.08.2025 putting the system into operation (edu.khogov.ru PDF). Confidence medium → high. Added densus.ru of 10.09.2026: Херсонская is excluded from using «Госуслуги Моя школа» alongside the regional system.
- **Checked:** corrected in the second round.

Образование»); operator Министерство образования и науки Челябинской области). Hosts: `sgo.edu-74.ru`, `es.edu-74.ru`, `poo.edu-74.ru`. Since: 2013 (ГИС «Образование»); primary through 2025/26. ## Москва

*Mandatory region.*

- **in use now: [МЭШ](mesh-moscow.md)** — «Московская электронная школа» (МЭШ) — электронный дневник (Департамент образования и науки города Москвы / Департамент информационных технологий города Москвы; apps published by «Центр Цифровизации Образования»). Hosts: `school.mos.ru`, `dnevnik.mos.ru`, `login.mos.ru`, `uchebnik.mos.ru`, `myschool.mos.ru`. Since: 2017/18 as the unified ОЭЖД on dnevnik.mos.ru (third-party API access to the old diary closed 1 September 2017); front end on school.mos.ru, dnevnik.mos.ru answers 307 to school.mos.ru (seen 2023). Confidence: high.
  school.mos.ru is a JS shell to WebFetch. API (reverse-engineered only): school.mos.ru /api/family/web/v1/*, /api/family/mobile/v1/*, /api/eventcalendar/*, /api/ej/*, /api/food/meals/*, token /v2/token/refresh, /v3/token/refresh, /v3/auth/sudir/auth; older ЭЖД surface dnevnik.mos.ru /core/api, /mobile/api, /jersey/api, /reports/api. Headers Authorization: Bearer <token> + Auth-Token, Profile-Id; cookie aupd_token. No official public API; ДИТ closed third-party access in 2017. No announcement of Moscow moving to ТОР «Госуслуги Моя школа»; the city is the platform owner that licenses МЭШ to other regions.
  - https://www.mos.ru/city/projects/mesh/ — «Зарегистрируйте себя и/или ученика на mos.ru» … «нажмите «Войти через mos.ru» и введите логин и пароль»; app «Дневник «МЭШ»» for iOS and Android; «С первой четверти 2026/2027 учебного года к «МЭШ» присоединяются еще пять новых субъектов России»
  - https://www.rustore.ru/catalog/app/ru.mes.dnevnik — «Дневник МЭШ» — official mobile application for Moscow schoolchildren and their parents; developer «Центр Цифровизации Образования»; version 3.86.25, updated 14 September 2026
  - https://habr.com/ru/articles/739190/ — «переходишь на URL dnevnik.mos.ru, происходит 307 статус код и кидает на school.mos.ru» (1 June 2023)
- **previous: МРКО — «Электронный дневник школьника» (Московский регистр качества об…** — МРКО — «Электронный дневник школьника» (Московский регистр качества образования) (Департамент образования города Москвы / ДИТ Москвы (МРКО)). Hosts: `mrko.mos.ru`, `pgu.mos.ru`. Since: until 2017 (retired with the move to ОЭЖД and mos.ru sign-in from 1 September 2017). Confidence: medium.
  HTML diary behind pgu.mos.ru; login + MD5 password. An earlier ЭЖД pilot also lived on schools.dnevnik.mos.ru (2015). Dead.
  - https://habr.com/ru/articles/335010/ — «МРКО — Электронный дневник школьника. Старый.»; ДИТ: migration to the unified mos.ru authorization, third-party developer access ceasing «с 1 сентября 2017 года» (article of 7 August 2017)
  - https://habr.com/ru/articles/323856/ — «MRKO login: GET mrko.mos.ru/dnevnik/services/index.php?login=&password=<md5> with Referer www.mos.ru/pgu/…/journal/ (2017)»
- **Open-source clients** aimed at this region or its platform: https://github.com/OctoDiary/OctoDiary-py (Python async library (PyPI octodiary 0.3.0) for МЭШ and «Моя школа»: login.mos.ru PKCE/SMS/proof-of-work login, /v3/auth/sudir/auth, family web+mobile API (2024-05)); https://github.com/Mag329/OctoDiary-py (fork of OctoDiary-py with newer routes (schedule, homework done, meals v2), Python (2026-02)); https://github.com/OctoDiary/OctoDiary-kt (Android client, Kotlin/Retrofit; api.md route table for МЭШ and «Моя школа» (2026-05)); https://github.com/OctoDiary/OctoDiary (Kotlin app repository of the OctoDiary organisation (2026-05)); https://github.com/OctoDiary/OctoDiary-tg (Telegram bot over OctoDiary-py, Python (2025-02)); https://github.com/RedGuyRu/DnevnikApi (JavaScript client for dnevnik.mos.ru/school.mos.ru (npm dnevnik-mos-ru-api), Puppeteer login on login.mos.ru incl. TOTP, 32 stars (2026-03)); https://github.com/RedGuyRu/DnevnikApiPython (Python port of the above (PyPI dnevnik-mos-ru-api), Playwright login (2025-11)); https://github.com/RedGuyRu/node-redguy-dnevnik-api (older Node wrapper via the author's proxy dapi.redguy.ru (2023))
- **Notes.** 2025/26 and 2026/27: МЭШ, web school.mos.ru (dnevnik.mos.ru redirects there), no migration announced. Sign-in: a mos.ru account (СУДИР, login.mos.ru) — the parent registers self and child on mos.ru and presses «Войти через mos.ru»; pupils have their own mos.ru accounts; login.mos.ru also offers QR login from the official app and second factors (SMS, e-mail, flash call, TOTP) and anti-bot proof-of-work, which is what open-source clients fight with; the resulting token is exchanged at school.mos.ru /v3/auth/sudir/auth. Mobile app: «Дневник МЭШ» (ru.mes.dnevnik, publisher «Центр Цифровизации Образования», v3.86.25 of 14 Sep 2026). No official API documentation; all routes are reverse-engineered. Before 2017: МРКО (mrko.mos.ru via pgu.mos.ru). GitHub repository search school.mos.ru re-read 2026-09-24 sorted by update: no repositories beyond those listed (plus an unrelated site blocker Vladislav3454/SchoolMosRu-blocker and a recon dump motesctf/school.mos.ru, excluded).
- **Checked:** deep check.

## Санкт-Петербург

*Mandatory region.*

- **in use now: [«Петербургское образование»](petersburg.md)** — «Петербургское образование. Электронный дневник» (Комитет по образованию Санкт-Петербурга; app and service published by СПб ГКУ «Управление информационных технологий и связи»; school-meal module via Netrika (/fps/api/netrika/...)). Hosts: `dnevnik2.petersburgedu.ru`, `petersburgedu.ru`. Since: about 2019/20 (dnevnik2 replaced the diary pages of petersburgedu.ru; dated from client history, not from an official announcement). Confidence: high.
  The city's own system; neither МЭШ nor a vendor product sold to other regions. petersburgedu.ru, dnevnik2.petersburgedu.ru and k-obr.spb.ru all answered 503 to WebFetch on 2026-09-24, so no city page was read; the store listing by the city's IT agency (updated 4 Sep 2026) is the evidence that it runs in 2026/27. No announcement of a move to ТОР «Госуслуги Моя школа» for 2026/27 was found, and Санкт-Петербург is not among the first-wave ТОР regions seen in this research. API: JSON under /api/ (api/user/auth/login, api/journal/person/related-child-list, api/journal/estimate/table, api/journal/schedule/list-by-education, api/journal/lesson/list-by-education, api/group/group/get-list-period), session in cookie X-JWT-Token re-issued on use; no official documentation. dnevnik.petersburgedu.ru, which the critic listed as unverified, does not exist: it has no DNS record (getaddrinfo ENOTFOUND on 2026-09-24), while dnevnik2.petersburgedu.ru and petersburgedu.ru resolve to 46.243.177.102–103. It is not a host of this system.
  - https://www.rustore.ru/catalog/app/ru.spb.iac.dnevnikspb_new — «Петербургское образование. Электронный Дневник» — «Электронный Дневник - узнавайте домашнее задание, следите за отметками и расписанием уроков, контролируйте рацион питания ваших детей, будьте в курсе времени прихода/выхода из школы.» Developer: Санкт-Петербургское государственное казенное учреждение «Управление информационных технологий и связи»; version 0.9.7, updated 4 September 2026; web: dnevnik2.petersburgedu.ru
  - https://github.com/newtover/dnevnik — «Если не хочется вводить пароль в чужие скрипты (или в случае авторизации через Госуслуги), куку можно скопировать из браузера» — cookie name X-JWT-Token on dnevnik2.petersburgedu.ru
  - https://github.com/mikhaillav/dnevnik2_docs — «swagger.yaml, POST api/user/auth/login: «this is a way how to login into journal, there is also "eisa", but i don't use it» (login type email \| eisa)»
- **alongside: [eSchool](eschool-center.md)** — eSchool «Электронная школа» (app.eschool.center) (ООО «ИТ-Стратегия» (commercial SaaS chosen per school)). Hosts: `app.eschool.center`. Confidence: low.
  Per-school use at some Petersburg lyceums alongside or instead of the city diary; not a regional system. Login and SHA-256 password hash, JSESSIONID cookie, JSON under /ec-server/.
  - https://github.com/reSchool-org/reSchool-flutter — «open-source eSchool client that ships bell presets for Petersburg schools ФМЛ №30 and ФМЛ №239»
- **alongside: [«Сетевой город»](netschool.md)** — «Сетевой город. Образование» — instance of one school (ФТШ им. Ж. И. Алфёрова / Иоффе) (ИрТех). Hosts: `netschool.school.ioffe.ru`. Confidence: low.
  One school's private NetSchool server; not the city system and possibly no longer used.
  - https://github.com/RunFMe/NetSchoolHelper — «JS helper for the NetSchool instance netschool.school.ioffe.ru (2017)»
- **alongside: [Дневник.ру](dnevnik-ru.md)** — Дневник.ру, chosen by individual schools (not the city system) (ООО «Дневник.ру» (registered in Санкт-Петербург)). Hosts: `dnevnik.ru`. Since: per school (ГБОУ школа № 617 says it has worked in it since 1 September 2009). Confidence: low.
  Two Petersburg schools name Дневник.ру on their own sites. ГБОУ школа № 617 (a state school) has an undated page with old-style links, so it may be stale, and it may run Дневник.ру beside the obligatory city diary rather than instead of it. The private school «Таурас» uses it for marks and the timetable (Q&A page, indexed September 2025). This is per-school use, not a city system. It does not rest on the Дневник.ру ЕСИА slug for region 78, which is not evidence of use. How many schools use it, and whether either still does in 2026/27, is not established.

## Ямало-Ненецкий автономный округ

*Mandatory region.*

- **in use now: [«Моя школа» (МЭШ)](mesh-myschool.md)** — «Московская электронная школа» (МЭШ) / «Моя школа» — Ямало-Ненецкий АО (web school.yanao.ru, entry /esia/) (ДИТ Москвы (МЭШ platform), under an agreement between Moscow and ЯНАО). Hosts: `school.yanao.ru`. Since: 2025/26 (officially connected from 1 September 2025; in all schools from November 2025). Confidence: high.
  ЯНАО is the sixth row of the table of regional sign-in pages on myschool.mos.ru, beside authedu.mosreg.ru, education.admoblkaluga.ru, ms-edu.tatar.ru, myschool.05edu.ru and myschool.72to.ru. school.yanao.ru resolves to 94.79.51.172–174, the same addresses as myschool.05edu.ru, myschool.72to.ru and education.admoblkaluga.ru. myschool.yanao.ru does not exist (NXDOMAIN). Tested in five schools in 2025 and planned for «вторую четверть учебного года 2025/2026». Salekhard schools held a week without homework on 5–9 November 2025 for the switch. For 2026/27 (AiF, 25.08.2026): «С нового учебного года платформа «Московская электронная школа» станет доступна во всех колледжах Ямала». The platform stays; no move away from it has been announced. Before МЭШ, the same hostname school.yanao.ru served an ЭлЖур instance (see the eljur entry).
  - https://myschool.mos.ru/help/instructions/mesh-id/authorization-esia/authorization-diaryreg/ — «Ямало-Ненецкий АО \| https://school.yanao.ru/esia/»
  - https://school.yanao.ru/manifest.json — «short_name «МЭШ»; «Московская электронная школа» — это проект для учителей, детей и родителей… (the same web manifest myschool.72to.ru serves)»
  - https://yamal.aif.ru/society/yamal-stanet-pervym-regionom-s-mesh-v-shkolah-i-kolledzhah — «На Ямале платформа работает во всех школах с ноября 2025 года»
- **alongside: [«Госуслуги Моя школа»](myschool-federal.md) as a front end** — мобильное приложение «Госуслуги. Моя школа» (Госуслуги / ТОР «Моя школа» (Google Play package ru.gosuslugi.school)). Hosts: `gosuslugi.ru/school`. Since: November 2025. Confidence: medium.
  In ЯНАО the family-facing name of the switch is «Госуслуги Моя школа»: regional news presents the move of ГИС «Образование Ямала» as a move to that app (yamal1.ru, 07.11.2025; ura.news, 14.11.2025). The data comes from the МЭШ-based regional node school.yanao.ru, whose web manifest is МЭШ's, so the app is a front end over the regional system. Host: gosuslugi.ru/school is the web version of «Госуслуги Моя школа», one address for every region. No ЯНАО source names it; the regional sources name only the app (RuStore, Google Play, App Store; package ru.gosuslugi.school), so the host is inferred from the platform, not read on a Yamal page. After the switch the app failed and was restored on 21.11.2025, with «Полное восстановление всех данных, включая оценки и расписание, может занять до 2-3 недель». The web diary proper is school.yanao.ru/esia/.
  - https://ura.news/news/1053033333 — «Школьников в ЯНАО переводят на новую платформу «Госуслуги. Моя школа». … Скачать приложение «Госуслуги. Моя школа» можно в RuStore, Google Play и App Store.»
  - https://ks-yanao.ru/news/obrazovanie/gosuslugi-moja-shkola-ozhili-na-jamale — «Ямальские родители и школьники вновь могут пользоваться мобильным приложением «Госуслуги. Моя школа».»
  - https://ura.news/news/1053038344 — «Работа мобильного приложения „Госуслуги. Моя школа" восстановлена.»
- **alongside: [ФГИС «Моя школа»](myschool-federal.md)** — ФГИС «Моя школа» (пилот 2022) (Минпросвещения / Минцифры). Hosts: `myschool.edu.ru`. Since: 2022/23 (pilot, 26 schools). Confidence: medium.
  ЯНАО was one of 15 pilot regions of the federal portal in 2022, and its regional ГИС was to be integrated with it. The portal is not a diary.
  - https://yamal-media.ru/news/jamal-stal-pilotnym-regionom-po-vnedreniju-superservisa-moja-shkola-k-nemu-podkljuchat-26-uchrezhdenij — «В автономном округе возможности государственной информационной системы на первом этапе используют 26 школ»
- **previous: [«Сетевой город»](netschool.md)** — ГИС «Образование Ямала» («Сетевой город. Образование») (ИРТех). Hosts: `sgo.yanao.ru`. Since: until November 2025. Confidence: high.

The legacy role is inferred from that migration news; nothing seen announces a shutdown date or archive access. The host still answers (a search snippet of sgo.yanao.ru/authorize/login reads «Образование Ямала … Для входа необходима подтвержденная учетная запись»), but WebFetch got 503 and the host is unreachable from here.
  - https://sever-press.ru/narrative/obschestvo/elektronnyj-dnevnik-v-janao-chto-eto-i-kak-im-polzovatsja/ — «Все школы ЯНАО подключены к государственной информационной системе (ГИС) «Образование Ямала».»

Its JS config carries yandex_messenger_stats {"from":"eljur"}, an msAuthUrl on the Microsoft tenant tbeduirkutsk.onmicrosoft.com with its redirect to oktb-irk.eljur.ru, and ajaxUrl "/ajaxauthorize". The page is titled «Образовательная платформа» and its footer reads «© 2021–2022 ЯНАО». Nothing found says how many schools used it. Google News searches in this round («ЭлЖур Ямал», «ЭлЖур ЯНАО электронный журнал», «ЭлЖур Салехард», «ЭлЖур Новый Уренгой») returned nothing about Yamal, so the row stays low. Last commit 2024-05-24, so it targets the legacy system.); https://github.com/daniilok21/Telegram-bot-with-NetSchool-API (Telegram bot on the NetSchool API. netschool_cap/regions.py maps «Ямало-Ненецкий автономный округ» to https://sgo.yanao.ru. Last commit 2026-06-19.); https://github.com/OctoDiary/OctoDiary (МЭШ/«Моя школа» client. RegionCode.Yamal("89") («ЯНАО») is in the region picker, but supportedRegionCodes covers only Moscow, MosReg and Tatarstan, so ЯНАО is listed and not supported.); https://github.com/SolomonNumb1/fgis-dnevnik-desktop (Lists ЯНАО with domain myschool.yanao.ru, which does not resolve (NXDOMAIN). The real МЭШ host is school.yanao.ru. The entry is wrong.)
- **Notes.** Summary for this region: ГИС «Образование Ямала» on «Сетевой город. Образование» (sgo.yanao.ru) through 2024/25. An МЭШ-based platform (school.yanao.ru, listed on myschool.mos.ru among the DIT «Моя школа» regions) is primary from 2025/26: officially from 1 September 2025, in all schools from November 2025. Mobile access is the «Госуслуги. Моя школа» app. For 2026/27 МЭШ is extended to all colleges of the region, and no further platform change is announced. WebSearch was exhausted, so the searches ran as WebFetch on search.brave.com (web and news). *.yanao.ru hosts (do.yanao.ru, sgo.yanao.ru, sgo89.ru, school news pages) answered 503 to WebFetch. Open-source client search: GitHub repository search for 'yanao', 'sgo.yanao' and 'school.yanao' found no diary repositories, and Sourcegraph found nothing for sgo.yanao.ru, school.yanao.ru or myschool.yanao. The clients listed were found in repositories already cloned for other regions. Sign-in: ЕСИА/Госуслуги (vesti-yamal: «Вход на платформу будет доступен … через Госуслуги»; the entry path is school.yanao.ru/esia/). The legacy sgo.yanao.ru also required a confirmed account («Для входа необходима подтвержденная учетная запись», search snippet). The МЭШ app bundle (AmetistYT/mesh_expressive, pkgs/mesh_expressive_js) has a YANAO region constant and a client id fgismobile_89 in regionIdToClientIdMap, but no ЯНАО host string. So the DIT app «Моя школа Дневник» (ru.mes.dnevnik.fgis) has ЯНАО wired as a region with its own OAuth client, while regional news points families to «Госуслуги. Моя школа». Extra searches this pass: GitHub repository search 'yanao' found 57 results, none a diary client (lQuickShadow/YANAO is a college help-desk app). Sourcegraph 'yanao.ru' matched only infoculture/govdomains.
